## Proposed Rule #10 - WhatsApp Configuration Guard
**Origin**: WhatsApp allowFrom policy blocking business/family communications; gateway instability disrupting operations.
**Consequence**: Critical business check-ins (Sammy sales) and family communications (Dad, Ebony) fail, breaking operational continuity.
**Guard**: 
1. After any WhatsApp gateway restart or configuration change, verify allowFrom policy includes all essential numbers: Sammy (+233575252253), John (+233233352252), Dad (+447983254695), Ebony (+233546081608), Mum (+233503654902).
2. Test delivery to at least one number from each category (business, family) after policy updates.
3. If gateway disconnections exceed 3 in 1 hour, alert #urgent immediately with manual intervention instructions.
**Implementation**: Add to security watchdog scan; include in post-configuration verification checklist.
**Status**: Proposed 2026-04-23.
$2 What Failed Today
- Cron job 'cron-status-report' failed with error: 'cron: job execution timed out'
- WhatsApp gateway experiencing intermittent disconnections (status 499/428)
- WhatsApp delivery failed for morning check-in to Sammy (pending manual intervention)
- WhatsApp sales check to Sammy blocked by allowFrom policy (RED FLAG)
- Family check-in to Dad blocked by allowFrom policy

**Consequence**: Critical business check-ins (Sammy sales) and family communications (Dad, Ebony) fail, breaking operational continuity.
**Guard**: 
1. After any WhatsApp gateway restart or configuration change, verify allowFrom policy includes all essential numbers: Sammy (+233575252253), John (+233233352252), Dad (+447983254695), Ebony (+233546081608), Mum (+233503654902).
2. Test delivery to at least one number from each category (business, family) after policy updates.
3. If gateway disconnections exceed 3 in 1 hour, alert #urgent immediately with manual intervention instructions.
**Implementation**: Add to security watchdog scan; include in post-configuration verification checklist.
**Status**: Proposed 2026-04-23.

**Consequence**: Critical business check-ins (Sammy sales) and family communications (Dad, Ebony) fail, breaking operational continuity.
**Guard**: 
1. After any WhatsApp gateway restart or configuration change, verify allowFrom policy includes all essential numbers: Sammy (+233575252253), John (+233233352252), Dad (+447983254695), Ebony (+233546081608), Mum (+233503654902).
2. Test delivery to at least one number from each category (business, family) after policy updates.
3. If gateway disconnections exceed 3 in 1 hour, alert #urgent immediately with manual intervention instructions.
**Implementation**: Add to security watchdog scan; include in post-configuration verification checklist.
**Status**: Proposed 2026-04-23.
$2 Key Insights
1. WhatsApp configuration is critically broken: allowFrom policy blocks essential business (Sammy) and family (Dad, Ebony) communications
2. Gateway instability (499/428 disconnections) suggests connectivity or authentication issues requiring immediate manual intervention
3. System can still perform internal audits and voice processing despite external channel failures
4. Urgent need to update WhatsApp allowFrom policy and troubleshoot gateway connectivity

**Consequence**: Critical business check-ins (Sammy sales) and family communications (Dad, Ebony) fail, breaking operational continuity.

3. If gateway disconnections exceed 3 in 1 hour, alert #urgent immediately with manual intervention instructions.
**Implementation**: Add to security watchdog scan; include in post-configuration verification checklist.
**Status**: Proposed 2026-04-23.
